1. **world.difficulty 字段**：创建 UI（vui WorldCreation.ts:24-25 四档已有 UI）写入 world；存档持久化（src/save/serialize.ts+SaveFile 按现有字段模式）；wld 导入回填（Main.wld 有难度位——查 WldImport 对应解析段，没有则 1:1 补）。原版枚举 Main.cs GameMode（0经典/1专家/2大师/3旅程）。

          const key = NATIVE_DROP_KEY[id] ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id];

          const v = dropVelocity(rng);
          const drop = game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, key, n, v.vx, v.vy);
          // 心(58)/星(184) 标记为 pickup：触碰回血/回蓝，不进背包
          if (drop) {
            if (id === 58) drop.pickup = 'heart';
            else if (id === 184) drop.pickup = 'star';
          }

    const pick = (pool: Array<{ id: number; tier: number; cond: string | null }>, minimumRarity: number): number => {

      if (p.thornsActive && attacker && !attacker.dead) {

        this.addDamageNumber(attacker.cx, attacker.y, reflect, false, '#80FF80');

      if (p.hp <= 0) {
        p.dead = true;
        p.deathsPve++;              // numberOfDeathsPVE（PL:53840,存档持久化）
        p.respawnTimer = 0;

  addDamageNumber(x: number, y: number, amount: number, crit: boolean, color = '#FFF') {

  /** 弹幕消亡钩子（Projectile.Kill 按弹型分发）：

   *  :28700-28711 衰减）。原版此处另有 Gore 1394-1397 碎片与尘 330——未移植，登记 */
  onProjectileDeath(projId: number, x: number, y: number, w = 0, h = 0) {
    if (projId !== 1085) return;
    const R = 150;
    const p = this.player;
    if (x - R < p.x + p.w && x + w + R > p.x && y - R < p.y + p.h && y + h + R > p.y) {
      p.luckState.brokenMirrorBadLuckTime = 25200;
    }
  }

  /** 吸血链（Projectile.vampireHeal :11439-11448 / ghostHeal :11404-11437 语义）：
   *  回复系数 = 吸血鬼飞刀 0.075 / 幽灵治疗 0.2−numHits×0.05（钳 >0，:11407-11411）；
   *  floor ≥1 且预算 >0 才生效（:11413/:11442），预算扣全额浮点（:11417/:11444）。
   *  ghost 侧旗标门在调用点（:12885 ghostHeal && !moonLeech）——此处双保险；
   *  非魔法弹预算照扣不回复（:11418-11421 原文顺序）。
   *  【近似登记】原版经治疗弹 305/298 飞达玩家后结算（含鬼疗跨队友选最缺血者
   *  :11424-11434）——本仓直回当前玩家；canGhostHeal 按恒 true；ghostHurt 未接 */
  applyLifeSteal(kind: 'vampire' | 'ghost', dmg: number, numHits: number, x: number, y: number, isMagic = false): number {
    const p = this.player;
    if (p.buffs.moonLeech) return 0;                       // 月噬 145 全禁（:12879/:12885）
    if (kind === 'ghost' && !p.equipStats.setBonus?.ghostHeal) return 0;
    const rate = kind === 'vampire' ? 0.075 : 0.2 - numHits * 0.05;
    if (rate <= 0) return 0;
    const heal = dmg * rate;
    const n = Math.floor(heal);
    if (n <= 0 || p.lifeSteal <= 0) return 0;
    p.lifeSteal -= heal;
    if (kind === 'ghost' && !isMagic) return 0;            // 预算已扣，非魔法不回复
    p.hp = Math.min(p.maxHp, p.hp + n);
    this.addDamageNumber(x, y - 10, n, false, '#40E080');
    return n;
  }

  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: Enemy) {
    // Boss 击败记账已由 Boss 逻辑块通用化(downed_<vanillaId>)
    void key;
    // 图鉴杀数（NPC.cs:79640-79643 NPCLoot→RegisterKill：netID 记账经 GetBestiaryCreditId
    // 归并变体/部位；IsNPCValidForBestiaryKillCredit 门在数据层。原版另有 GetWereThereAnyInteractions
    // 门（雕像产怪无玩家交互不计）——本仓 hurt 无伤害源参数，近似为全部计入，仅环境击杀的
    // 雕像怪是偏差面）
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null
      && isNpcValidForBestiaryKillCredit(enemy.vanillaId, !!enemy.def.boss)) {
      this.world.bestiary.registerKill(enemy.vanillaNetId !== 0 && enemy.vanillaNetId != null
        ? enemy.vanillaNetId : enemy.vanillaId);
    }
    // 史莱姆雨击杀推进（NPC.cs:79883-79898：slimeRainNPC 击杀 → killCount≥150 且场上无王 → 召王）
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null && this.world.weather.slimeRain) {
      if (this.world.weather.advanceSlimeRainKill(enemy.vanillaId) && !this.boss) {
        this.summonBoss('king_slime');
      }
    }
    // 月事件计分（NPC.cs CheckProgressFrostMoon/PumpkinMoon :79245/:79487）：
    // 必须在掉落结算之后同帧调用（Enemy.hurt 尾部 onEnemyKilled 即 NPCLoot 之后，
    // 对齐原版 checkDead NPCLoot → CheckProgress 顺序 NPC.cs:79020-79021），
    // 保证本波掉落 gate 用的是进波前的 wave。进波公告由此处广播
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null && moonEventActive(this.world)) {
      const diff = this.player.appearance?.difficulty ?? 0;
      const ann = addMoonEventKill(this.world, enemy.vanillaId, diff >= 2, diff >= 3);
      if (ann) this.newText(invasionWaveText(ann), 175, 75, 255);
    }
    // 星璇塔事件（NPC.cs:80080-80146）：
    //  本组专属怪死亡 → 发射 629 TowerDamageBolt → 命中塔盾 -1（归属表 LunarEvent.MINION_TOWER）；
    //  塔真死（180t 死亡演出后）→ downedTower_* + UpdateLunarApocalypse + MessageLunarApocalypse。
    //  与月事件计分同位（掉落结算之后同帧，对齐原版 checkDead 尾段顺序）
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null) {
      if (isTowerId(enemy.vanillaId)) {
        this.onLunarTowerKilled(enemy.vanillaId);
      } else if (this.world.lunarEvent.active && isTowerMinion(enemy.vanillaId)) {
        this.onLunarMinionKilled(enemy);
      }
    }
    // 入侵击杀扣分（NPC.cs:79021-79054：组号匹配当前入侵类型才计分，加权扣减）
    if (enemy && this.world.invasionType > 0 && enemy.vanillaId != null) {
      const group = INVASION_GROUP[enemy.vanillaId];
      if (group && group === this.world.invasionType) {
        this.world.invasionSize = Math.max(0, this.world.invasionSize - (KILL_WEIGHT[enemy.vanillaId] ?? 1));
      }
    }
    // 旧日军团计分（DD2Event.CheckProgress :344-470 + AnnounceGoblinDeath :733-736）：
    // 与月事件同位（掉落结算之后同帧，对齐原版 checkDead 尾段顺序）
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null) {
      const oa = this.world.oldOnesArmy;
      if (oa.ongoing) {
        // 小妖精三档死亡登记尸点（黑暗魔法师 RaiseGoblins 的复活源，NPC.cs:80040-80052
        // switch case 552/553/554——仅 Goblin T1/T2/T3；555+ 与 551 不登记）
        if (enemy.vanillaId >= 552 && enemy.vanillaId <= 554) {
          dd2AnnounceGoblinDeath(oa, enemy.cx, enemy.y + enemy.h);
        }
        const diff = this.player.appearance?.difficulty ?? 0;
        const prog = dd2CheckProgress(oa, enemy.vanillaId, diff >= 2, this.betsyLifeRatio());
        if (prog.waveComplete) {
          this.newText(Lang.text(prog.waveComplete), 175, 55, 255);   // INFO_NEW_WAVE_COLOR
          if (prog.medals > 0) this.dd2Host().dropAtCrystal(3817, prog.medals);
        }
        if (prog.victoryScene) this.startDD2VictoryScene();
        // 击杀推进中的魔力配额掉落（ShouldDropCrystals :799-927——波内按击杀进度放出）
        if (dd2ShouldDropCrystals(oa, this.betsyLifeRatio())) {
          this.dd2Host().dropAtCrystal(3822, 1);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /** Betsy 血量比（D3 第 7 波进度 = 100×已损血比，DD2Event.cs:1503-1513；无 Betsy → null） */
  private betsyLifeRatio(): number | null {
    const betsy = (this.entities.enemies as Enemy[]).find((e) => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === 551);
    return betsy ? betsy.hp / betsy.maxHp : null;
  }

	public static bool masterMode => Difficulty >= GameDifficultyLevel.Master;

	public static bool expertMode => Difficulty >= GameDifficultyLevel.Expert;

	public static float Difficulty
	{
		get
		{
			float num = GameDifficultyLevel.Classic;
			if (ActiveWorldFileData != null)
			{
				if (_gameModeDifficultyOverride.HasValue)
				{
					num = _gameModeDifficultyOverride.Value;
				}
				else if (GameMode == 1)
				{
					num = GameDifficultyLevel.Expert;
				}
				else if (GameMode == 2)
				{
					num = GameDifficultyLevel.Master;
				}
				if (getGoodWorld)
				{
					num += 1f;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}
	}

	public long DropCoins()
	{
		IEntitySource itemSource_Death = GetItemSource_Death();
		long num = 0L;
		Item[] array = inventory;
		foreach (Item item in array)
		{
			if (item.IsACoin)
			{
				int num2 = item.stack / 2;
				if (Main.expertMode)
				{
					num2 = item.stack / 4;
				}
				if (Main.masterMode)
				{
					num2 = 0;
				}
				int num3 = item.stack - num2;
				if (item.type == 71)
				{
					num += num3;
				}
				if (item.type == 72)
				{
					num += (long)num3 * 100L;
				}
				if (item.type == 73)
				{
					num += (long)num3 * 10000L;
				}
				if (item.type == 74)
				{
					num += (long)num3 * 1000000L;
				}
				TryDroppingSingleItem(itemSource_Death, item, num3);
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	public void TryDroppingSingleItem(IEntitySource source, Item theItem)
	{
		TryDroppingSingleItem(source, theItem, theItem.stack);
	}

	public void TryDroppingSingleItem(IEntitySource source, Item theItem, int stack)
	{
		if (stack > 0 && Main.netMode != 1)
		{
			int num = Item.NewItem(source, (int)position.X, (int)position.Y, width, height, theItem.type, stack, noBroadcast: true, theItem.prefix);
			WorldItem obj = Main.item[num];
			obj.velocity.Y = (float)Main.rand.Next(-20, 1) * 0.2f;
			obj.velocity.X = (float)Main.rand.Next(-20, 21) * 0.2f;
			NetMessage.SendData(21, -1, -1, null, num);
		}
		theItem.stack -= stack;
		if (theItem.stack <= 0)
		{
			theItem.TurnToAir(fullReset: true);
		}
	}

// 世界门面：TileStore + 元数据 + 实体容器 + 昼夜时钟
import { TileStore } from './TileStore';
import { WeatherState, applyWeatherSave, weatherToSave, type WeatherSave } from './weather/Weather';
import { T, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { newMoonEventState } from './MoonEvent';
import { newOldOnesArmyState } from './OldOnesArmy';
import { newLunarEventState } from './LunarEvent';
import type { FurnitureItemRecord } from './FurnitureItems';
import { BestiaryTracker, type BestiarySave } from '../data/Bestiary';

export interface ChestData { x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>; locked?: boolean; }

export class Clock {
  /** 昼夜边界（1:1 Main.time：昼 4:30AM-7:30PM=54000t、夜 32400t；0=午夜线性 24h） */
  static readonly DAWN = 4.5 / 24;   // 0.1875
  static readonly DUSK = 19.5 / 24;  // 0.8125
  timeOfDay: number = 8.25 / 24;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始 8:15AM = Main.time 13500）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 日食旗标（Main.eclipse，运行时态不进存档）：黎明 roll（Main.cs:64898，
   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），
   *  夜幕清除（Main.cs:64721）——全天持续，只影响白天刷怪段 */
  eclipse: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK - 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK + 0.04) return 1 - (t - (Clock.DUSK - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK + 0.04 || t < Clock.DAWN - 0.04) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - (Clock.DAWN - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }
  get hourFloat(): number { return this.timeOfDay * 24; }
}

export class World {
  store: TileStore;
  /** 本帧 SceneMetrics 快照（Game 每帧写；Enemy 判 zoneGraveyard 等只读用） */
  scene: import('./SceneMetrics').SceneFlags | null = null;
  /** 彩蛋种子旗标（SeedEasterEggs.parseSeed；血月 1/6 十周年 / drunkWorld 黎明翻转用。
   *  生成期灌入 + 存档 header.seedFlags 持久化；wld 导入恒 {}） */
  seedFlags: import('./gen/SeedEasterEggs').SeedFlags = {};
  name: string;
  seed: number;
  spawnX = 0; spawnY = 0;
  groundLevel = 0; rockLevel = 0;   // tile 坐标
  /** 地狱顶（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200；TerrainPass 设定，SceneMetrics/BGM/背景共用） */
  lavaLine = 0;
  clock: Clock;
  /** 天气系统（WeatherState 状态机；存档/wld 导入经 applyWeatherSave 回填） */
  weather = new WeatherState();
  applyWeather(s: Partial<WeatherSave>) { applyWeatherSave(this.weather, s); }
  chests: ChestData[] = [];
  /** 墓碑碑文（原版 Sign 系统的最小子集）：锚点 = 墓碑 tile 左上格 */

  shimmerY = 0;

  dungeonX = 0;            // 地牢位置
  /** 地牢入口地表 Y（原版 Main.dungeonY：CheckToSpawnDungeonEnemies 要求玩家在其 +40 格以下才刷地牢怪）。
   *  缺省 0=未知，使用处以 groundLevel 回退 */
  dungeonY = 0;
  jungleX = 0;             // 丛林位置
  // ---- 南瓜月/霜月事件（原版 Main.pumpkinMoon/snowMoon + NPC.waveNumber 三字段）----
  // 运行时状态，**不进存档**：原版这些字段均不写 WorldFile（主菜单即重置 Main.cs:48332），
  // serialize/fromPacket 不碰本字段；读档后 kind=0 即无事件（对齐原版）
  moonEvent = newMoonEventState();
  // ---- 旧日军团（原版 DD2Event 全静态位；召唤入口 = 永恒水晶座 466 右键）----
  // 运行时状态不存档（对齐原版：仅 DownedInvasionT1-3 随 WorldFile 存 → flags）。
  // 纯逻辑在 world/OldOnesArmy.ts（DD2Event.cs 1:1）；Game 接线 + AI 依赖注入
  // DD2_EVENT_HOOKS（bossAI_dd2.ts 占位）

  // 运行时状态：downedTower*/towerActive*/lunarApocalypseUp 三类 bool 走 flags 进存档
  // （WorldFile.cs:1352-1360）；盾值与月总倒计时**不存档**（读档 TowerActive=true 重置满盾，
  // WorldFile.cs:2240-2255），读档回填见 LunarEvent.applyLunarSave
  lunarEvent = newLunarEventState();
  // ---- 入侵系统持久化状态（原版 WorldFile.cs:1306-1339 存档五元组）----
  /** 当前入侵类型（InvasionID：0=无 1=哥布林 2=雪人军团 3=海盗；4=火星后续） */
  invasionType = 0;
  /** 剩余入侵点数（击杀扣减，≤0 即胜利） */
  invasionSize = 0;
  /** 入侵开始总点数（进度条分母） */
  invasionSizeStart = 0;
  /** 入侵前线图块 X（向 spawnX 推进；启动时 = 世界左右边缘） */
  invasionX = 0;
  /** 入侵冷却天数（黎明 -1；1.4.5.6 事实闲置，仅存档位） */
  invasionDelay = 0;
  /** TreeTops 13 区域变体（v≥211 wld 权威；索引 0-3 森林/5 丛林/6 雪/7 神圣） */
  treeTops: number[] = [];

  exploredVersion = 0;
  /** 最近一次 markExplored 新点亮格的包围盒（tile 坐标；null = 无新探索）。
   *  渲染端雾画布按此做脏矩形增量更新——旧版无条件 bump 版本导致每 15 tick
   *  整幅重建 4200×1200 雾画布（20MB 分配 + 500 万格循环 ≈ 672ms 长任务），
   *  首次导入大世界开地图时主线程持续阻塞 → 白屏闪烁 + 标签页 OOM 崩溃 */
  exploredDirty: { x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number } | null = null;
  markExplored(cx: number, cy: number, radius: number) {
    const st = this.store;
    const x0 = Math.max(0, cx - radius), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, cx + radius);
    const y0 = Math.max(0, cy - radius), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, cy + radius);
    let changed = false;
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
        const i = y * st.w + x;
        if (!this.explored[i] && (x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2 <= radius * radius) {
          this.explored[i] = 1;
          changed = true;
          // 脏包围盒与新点亮格取并（渲染帧间多次 mark 不丢更新）
          const d = this.exploredDirty;
          if (d) { if (x < d.x0) d.x0 = x; if (x > d.x1) d.x1 = x; if (y < d.y0) d.y0 = y; if (y > d.y1) d.y1 = y; }
          else this.exploredDirty = { x0: x, y0: y, x1: x, y1: y };
        }
      }
    }
    if (changed) this.exploredVersion++;
  }

  constructor(w: number, h: number, seed: number, name = '新世界') {
    this.store = new TileStore(w, h);
    this.explored = new Uint8Array(w * h);
    this.seed = seed;
    this.name = name;
    // 1 游戏日 = 30 现实分钟（24→40 后折中）
    this.clock = new Clock(24 * 60 * 1000); // 1:1 全周期 24min（昼 15/夜 9）
  }

  /** 从 worker 数据包重建（buffer 已 transfer 移交，零拷贝包装）。
   *  explored 未包含在包内（saveGame 不持久化、新生成/读档均全零）时按全零分配 */
  static fromPacket(p: import('../workers/protocol').WorldPacket): World {
    const w = new World(p.w, p.h, p.seed, p.name);
    w.store = new TileStore(p.w, p.h, p.buf);
    w.explored = p.buf.explored ? new Uint8Array(p.buf.explored) : new Uint8Array(p.w * p.h);
    w.spawnX = p.spawnX; w.spawnY = p.spawnY;
    w.groundLevel = p.groundLevel; w.rockLevel = p.rockLevel; w.lavaLine = p.lavaLine;
    w.crimson = p.crimson; w.dungeonX = p.dungeonX; w.dungeonY = p.dungeonY; w.jungleX = p.jungleX;
    w.exploredVersion = p.exploredVersion;
    w.clock.timeOfDay = p.clock.timeOfDay;
    w.clock.dayCount = p.clock.dayCount;
    if (p.clock.bloodMoon !== undefined) w.clock.bloodMoon = p.clock.bloodMoon;
    if (p.clock.eclipse !== undefined) w.clock.eclipse = p.clock.eclipse;
    if (p.clock.moonPhase !== undefined) w.clock.moonPhase = p.clock.moonPhase;
    if (p.seedFlags) w.seedFlags = { ...p.seedFlags };
    if (p.moonType !== undefined) w.moonType = p.moonType;
    if (p.invasion) { w.invasionType = p.invasion.type; w.invasionSize = p.invasion.size;
      w.invasionSizeStart = p.invasion.sizeStart; w.invasionX = p.invasion.x; w.invasionDelay = p.invasion.delay; }
    if (p.altarCount !== undefined) w.altarCount = p.altarCount;
    if (p.weather) w.applyWeather(p.weather);
    if (p.bestiary) w.applyBestiary(p.bestiary);
    w.pendingTownNpcs = p.pendingTownNpcs;
    w.chests = p.chests;
    w.signs = p.signs;
    w.furnitureItems = p.furnitureItems ?? [];
    w.trees = p.trees;
    w.flags = p.flags;
    w.treeX = p.treeX; w.treeStyle = p.treeStyle; w.treeTops = p.treeTops;
    w.shimmerX = p.shimmerX ?? 0; w.shimmerY = p.shimmerY ?? 0;
    return w;
  }

  get w() { return this.store.w; }
  get h() { return this.store.h; }
  get tiles() { return this.store; }

  /** 破坏一个 tile（含 framed 物体的锚点处理由调用方负责），返回掉落 item key */
  breakTileAt(x: number, y: number): string | null {
    const t = this.store.get(x, y);
    if (t === 0) return null;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
    this.store.setTile(x, y, 0);
    // v_ 图块无显式 drop → 回退同名放置物品(阶段5 自动注册)。
    // drop:null = 显式无掉落(原版装饰类)——?? 会把 null 也走兜底,必须显式判 undefined
    return d.drop === undefined ? (d.key.startsWith('v_') ? 'place_' + d.key : null) : d.drop;
  }

  isSolidPx(px: number, py: number): boolean {
    return this.store.isSolid(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16));
  }

  /** 液体量（0-255）按像素坐标 */
  liquidAt(px: number, py: number): number {
    return this.store.liquid[this.store.idx(Math.floor(px / 16), Math.floor(py / 16))];
  }

  registerTree(x: number, y: number, h: number) {
    this.trees.push({ x, y, h });
  }
}

export { T };

```

12	class ByteWriter {
13	  buf = new Uint8Array(1 << 16);
14	  len = 0;
15	  private grow(need: number) {
16	    if (this.len + need <= this.buf.length) return;
17	    let cap = this.buf.length;
18	    while (cap < this.len + need) cap *= 2;
19	    const nb = new Uint8Array(cap);
20	    nb.set(this.buf.subarray(0, this.len));
21	    this.buf = nb;
22	  }
23	  varint(v: number) {
24	    v = v < 0 ? 0 : v;
25	    this.grow(5);  // 32 位 varint 至多 5 字节
26	    while (v >= 0x80) {
27	      this.buf[this.len++] = (v & 0x7f) | 0x80;
28	      v >>>= 7;
29	    }
30	    this.buf[this.len++] = v;
31	  }
32	  bytes(): Uint8Array {
33	    return this.buf.subarray(0, this.len);
34	  }
35	}
36	
37	export function bytesToB64(u8: Uint8Array): string {
38	  // 分块拼接(8192)避免逐字符 += 的 ConsString 链;输出与逐字符版一致
39	  let s = '';
40	  const CH = 8192;
41	  for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i += CH) {
42	    s += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, u8.subarray(i, Math.min(i + CH, u8.length)) as unknown as number[]);
43	  }
44	  return btoa(s);
45	}

156	  altarCount?: number;
157	  /** 图鉴三桶（杀数/遇数/聊天）——data/Bestiary.ts BestiarySave */
158	  bestiary?: import('../data/Bestiary').BestiarySave;
159	}
160	
161	export function rlePairs(values: ArrayLike<number>): Uint8Array {
162	  const out = new ByteWriter();
163	  const n = values.length;
164	  let i = 0;
165	  while (i < n) {
166	    const v = values[i];
167	    let run = 1;
168	    while (i + run < n && values[i + run] === v && run < 0x7fffffff) run++;
169	    out.varint(run - 1);
170	    out.varint(v);
171	    i += run;
172	  }
173	  return out.bytes();
174	}
175	
176	/** RLE tiles。stableMap 非空时逐元素映射为稳定 id(v3 存档路径) */
177	export function rleTiles(st: { type: Uint16Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array }, stableMap?: Uint16Array): Uint8Array {
178	  const out = new ByteWriter();
179	  const n = st.type.length;
180	  let i = 0;
181	  while (i < n) {
182	    const raw = st.type[i];
183	    const t = stableMap ? (raw === 0 ? 0 : stableMap[raw]) : raw;
184	    const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
185	    let run = 1;
186	    // 游程比较必须 raw 对 raw（t 是映射后的稳定 id，与原始 id 比较恒不等 →
187	    // 自定义 tile（stable≠internal）游程全断，4200x1200 规则地形 17B 膨胀到 21MB）
188	    while (i + run < n && st.type[i + run] === raw && st.frameX[i + run] === fx && st.frameY[i + run] === fy) run++;
189	    out.varint(run - 1);
190	    out.varint(t);
191	    if (t !== 0) {
192	      out.varint(fx);
193	      out.varint(fy);
194	    }
195	    i += run;
196	  }
197	  return out.bytes();
198	}
199	
200	/** 存档序列化主体。幽灵净化在视图上就地执行(主线程=活存储原语义;
201	 * worker=副本,净化不影响游戏内状态,存档字节一致) */
202	export function serializeSave(
203	  st: SaveStoreView, meta: SaveMeta,
204	  player: SaveData['player'], playTimeMs: number,
205	  townNpcs?: SaveData['npcs'],
206	  events?: SaveData['events'],
207	): string {
208	  // 幽灵净化:存档格式不携带 active 位(加载时 type≠0 即激活),
209	  // 非活性格上的残留 type 必须归零,否则重读后全部变成浮空实体块
210	  for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
211	    if (!st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) st.type[i] = 0;
212	  }
213	  // 物品 id → 稳定 id(箱子/背包/储物;量小直接遍历)
214	  const mapItem = (it: { id: number; stack: number } | null) =>
215	    it ? { id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[it.id] ?? 0, stack: it.stack } : null;
216	  const data: SaveData = {
217	    format: 'sandboxworld.save',
218	    version: 3,
219	    header: {
220	      name: meta.name, seed: meta.seed, width: meta.w, height: meta.h,
221	      spawn: [meta.spawnX, meta.spawnY], groundLevel: meta.groundLevel, rockLevel: meta.rockLevel,
222	      lavaLine: meta.lavaLine,
223	      treeX: meta.treeX, treeStyle: meta.treeStyle, treeTops: meta.treeTops,
224	      crimson: meta.crimson, dungeonX: meta.dungeonX, dungeonY: meta.dungeonY, jungleX: meta.jungleX,
225	      created: Date.now(), playTimeMs,
226	      moonType: meta.moonType ?? 0,
227	      seedFlags: meta.seedFlags,
228	    },
229	    clock: {
230	      timeOfDay: meta.timeOfDay, dayCount: meta.dayCount,
231	      bloodMoon: meta.bloodMoon ?? false, eclipse: meta.eclipse ?? false,
232	      moonPhase: meta.moonPhase ?? 0,
233	    },
234	    invasion: meta.invasion ?? { type: 0, size: 0, sizeStart: 0, x: 0, delay: 0 },
235	    altarCount: meta.altarCount ?? 0,
236	    tiles: bytesToB64(rleTiles(st, TILE_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL)),
237	    walls: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wall)),
238	    liquid: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquid)),
239	    liquidType: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquidType)),
240	    wire: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wire)),
241	    blocks: bytesToB64(rlePairs(combineBlocks(st.half, st.slope))),
242	    chests: meta.chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, locked: c.locked || undefined, items: c.items.map(mapItem) })),
243	    player: { ...player, inventory: player.inventory.map(mapItem), banks: player.banks?.map((b) => b.map(mapItem)) },
244	    npcs: townNpcs ?? [],   // 城镇 NPC 段(WorldFile.cs:1703-1750 NPC 段;曾硬编码 [] 断链)
245	    signs: meta.signs,
246	    // 挂物族：存放物 id 走与 chests 相同的稳定 id 映射（mapItem 同构，保留 prefix）
247	    furniture: meta.furniture?.map((e) => ({
248	      x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind,
249	      slots: e.slots.map((s) => (s ? { id: s.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[s.id] ?? 0, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix } : null)),
250	    })),
251	    flags: meta.flags,
252	    weather: meta.weather,
253	    events,
254	    bestiary: meta.bestiary,
255	    idScheme: 'sheet',
256	  };
257	  return JSON.stringify(data);
258	}
259	
260	/** half + slope → 单字节 blockType(原版 Tile.blockType: 0=整砖 1=半砖 2-5=slope1-4) */
261	function combineBlocks(half: Uint8Array, slope: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
262	  const out = new Uint8Array(half.length);
263	  for (let i = 0; i < half.length; i++) {
264	    out[i] = half[i] ? 1 : (slope[i] >= 1 && slope[i] <= 4 ? slope[i] + 1 : 0);
265	  }
266	  return out;
267	}
268	

```

1	// 多世界存储：KvStore 索引 + 每世界一条 SaveData JSON。
2	// index: sandboxworld.worlds.index → WorldMeta[]
3	// data : sandboxworld.world.<id>   → SaveFile JSON 全文
4	import { kvGet, kvSet, kvHas, kvDelete } from './KvStore';
5	
6	const INDEX_KEY = 'sandboxworld.worlds.index';
7	const QUICK_SAVE_KEY = 'sandboxworld.quicksave';
8	const dataKey = (id: number) => `sandboxworld.world.${id}`;
9	
10	export interface WorldMeta {
11	  id: number;
12	  name: string;
13	  seed: string;          // 用户输入的 seed 文本（原样保存）
14	  w: number; h: number;
15	  difficulty: number;    // 0 经典 1 专家 2 大师 3 旅程
16	  evil: -1 | 0 | 1;      // -1 随机 0 腐化 1 猩红
17	  createdAt: number;
18	  lastPlayed: number;
19	  playTimeMs: number;
20	  favorite: boolean;
21	}
22	
23	let nextId = 1;
24	
25	export class WorldStore {
26	  private index: WorldMeta[] = [];
27	  private loaded = false;
28	
29	  async ensureLoaded() {
30	    if (this.loaded) return;
31	    const raw = await kvGet(INDEX_KEY);
32	    if (raw) {
33	      try {
34	        const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
35	        if (Array.isArray(arr)) this.index = arr.filter((x) => x && typeof x.id === 'number');
36	      } catch { /* 损坏索引当空 */ }
37	    }
38	    // 迁移：旧版单槽 quicksave → 世界 id=1（幂等）
39	    if (this.index.length === 0 && !raw) {
40	      if (await kvHas(QUICK_SAVE_KEY)) {
41	        const text = await kvGet(QUICK_SAVE_KEY);
42	        if (text) {
43	          try {
44	            const h = (JSON.parse(text) as { header?: { name?: string; seed?: number; width?: number; height?: number; created?: number; playTimeMs?: number } }).header;
45	            if (h) {
46	              await kvSet(dataKey(1), text);
47	              this.index.push({
48	                id: 1, name: h.name ?? '旧世界', seed: String(h.seed ?? ''),
49	                w: h.width ?? 4200, h: h.height ?? 1200,
50	                difficulty: 0, evil: -1,
51	                createdAt: h.created ?? Date.now(), lastPlayed: h.created ?? Date.now(),
52	                playTimeMs: h.playTimeMs ?? 0, favorite: false,
53	              });
54	              await this.saveIndex();
55	              nextId = 2;
56	            }
57	          } catch { /* quicksave 损坏则跳过 */ }
58	        }
59	      }
60	    }
61	    for (const m of this.index) if (m.id >= nextId) nextId = m.id + 1;
62	    this.loaded = true;
63	  }
64	
65	  private async saveIndex() {
66	    await kvSet(INDEX_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.index));
67	  }
68	
69	  /** 1.4.5.6 UIWorldSelect 排序键序：收藏 → 最后游玩 → 名称 → 文件名(即 id) */
70	  list(): WorldMeta[] {
71	    return [...this.index].sort((a, b) =>
72	      (Number(b.favorite) - Number(a.favorite))
73	      || (b.lastPlayed - a.lastPlayed)
74	      || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh')
75	      || (a.id - b.id));
76	  }
77	
78	  async register(m: Omit<WorldMeta, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'lastPlayed' | 'playTimeMs' | 'favorite'>): Promise<WorldMeta> {
79	    await this.ensureLoaded();
80	    const meta: WorldMeta = { ...m, id: nextId++, createdAt: Date.now(), lastPlayed: Date.now(), playTimeMs: 0, favorite: false };
81	    this.index.push(meta);
82	    await this.saveIndex();
83	    return meta;
84	  }
85	
86	  /** 保存世界数据（同时刷新 meta 的游玩时间/时长） */
87	  async save(meta: WorldMeta, saveJson: string, playTimeMs: number) {
88	    meta.lastPlayed = Date.now();
89	    meta.playTimeMs = playTimeMs;
90	    await kvSet(dataKey(meta.id), saveJson);
91	    await this.saveIndex();
92	  }
93	
94	  async load(meta: WorldMeta): Promise<string | null> {
95	    return kvGet(dataKey(meta.id));
96	  }
97	
98	  async delete(id: number) {
99	    await this.ensureLoaded();
100	    this.index = this.index.filter((x) => x.id !== id);
101	    await this.saveIndex();
102	    await kvDelete(dataKey(id));
103	  }
104	
105	  async duplicate(meta: WorldMeta): Promise<WorldMeta | null> {
106	    const text = await this.load(meta);
107	    if (!text) return null;
108	    const copy = await this.register({ ...meta, name: `${meta.name} 副本` });
109	    await kvSet(dataKey(copy.id), text);
110	    return copy;
111	  }
112	
113	  async setFavorite(id: number, v: boolean) {
114	    const m = this.index.find((x) => x.id === id);
115	    if (!m) return;
116	    m.favorite = v;
117	    await this.saveIndex();
118	  }
119	}
120

18	function b64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
19	  const s = atob(b64);
20	  const u8 = new Uint8Array(s.length);
21	  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u8[i] = s.charCodeAt(i);
22	  return u8;
23	}

25	function readVarint(u8: Uint8Array, pos: { i: number }): number {
26	  let v = 0, shift = 0;
27	  while (pos.i < u8.length) {
28	    const b = u8[pos.i++];
29	    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
30	    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
31	    shift += 7;
32	  }
33	  return v;
34	}

183	  world.furnitureItems = furnitureFromSave(
184	    data.furniture,
185	    (id) => (v3 ? ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.get(id) ?? 0 : id),
186	  );
187	
188	  const st = world.store;
189	  // tiles(v3:RLE 里的 type 是稳定 id,查 INTERNAL_OF_STABLE 还原;v2 旧档=内部下标直读)
190	  {
191	    const v3 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 3;
192	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.tiles);
193	    const pos = { i: 0 };
194	    let i = 0;
195	    while (i < st.type.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
196	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
197	      const tRaw = readVarint(u8, pos);
198	      const t = v3 ? (tRaw === 0 ? 0 : TILE_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE[tRaw]) : tRaw;
199	      let fx = 0, fy = 0;
200	      if (t !== 0) {
201	        fx = readVarint(u8, pos);
202	        fy = readVarint(u8, pos);
203	      }
204	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.type.length; k++, i++) {
205	        st.type[i] = t; st.flags[i] = t !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
206	        st.frameX[i] = fx; st.frameY[i] = fy;
207	      }
208	    }
209	  }
210	  // walls（version<2 的旧档墙值迁移：1=土→2, 2=石→1, 3=木→4（现为原版 id））
211	  {
212	    const legacy = (data.version ?? 1) < 2;
213	    const migrate = (v: number) => (legacy ? (v === 1 ? 2 : v === 2 ? 1 : v === 3 ? 4 : v) : v);
214	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.walls);
215	    const pos = { i: 0 };
216	    let i = 0;
217	    while (i < st.wall.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
218	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
219	      const v = migrate(readVarint(u8, pos));
220	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.wall.length; k++, i++) st.wall[i] = v;
221	    }
222	  }
223	  // wire 电路位（旧存档缺此字段 → 全 0 = 无导线，缺字段容忍同 liquidType）
224	  {
225	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.wire ?? '');
226	    const pos = { i: 0 };
227	    let i = 0;
228	    while (i < st.wire.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
229	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
230	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
231	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.wire.length; k++, i++) st.wire[i] = v;
232	    }
233	  }
234	  // half/slope(旧存档缺 → 全整砖;v3+ 语义)
235	  {
236	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.blocks ?? '');
237	    const pos = { i: 0 };
238	    let i = 0;
239	    while (i < st.half.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
240	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
241	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
242	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.half.length; k++, i++) {
243	        st.half[i] = v === 1 ? 1 : 0;
244	        st.slope[i] = v >= 2 && v <= 5 ? v - 1 : 0;
245	      }
246	    }
247	  }
248	  // liquid type（旧存档缺此字段 → 全 0，由液体模拟按 1=水 补全）
249	  {
250	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.liquidType ?? '');
251	    const pos = { i: 0 };
252	    let i = 0;
253	    while (i < st.liquidType.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
254	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
255	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
256	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.liquidType.length; k++, i++) st.liquidType[i] = v;
257	    }
258	  }
259	  // liquid
260	  {
261	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.liquid);
262	    const pos = { i: 0 };
263	    let i = 0;
264	    while (i < st.liquid.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
265	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
266	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
267	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.liquid.length; k++, i++) st.liquid[i] = v;
268	    }
269	  }
270	  return { world, player: data.player, data };
271	}

  /** Announcement 类消息便捷封装(NPC.cs/WorldGen.cs BroadcastChatMessage 语义) */
  announce(key: string, who: string, r: number, g: number, b: number): void {
    this.newText(Lang.text(key, who), r, g, b);
  }

  // ================= 重生 =================

  /** 玩家死亡瞬间：原版死亡文本 + 红色粉碎粒子 + 掉落物理墓碑（Player.DropTombstone） */
  private _deathHandled = false;
  private handlePlayerDeath() {
    const p = this.player;
    // 死亡文本（Lang.CreateDeathMessage 1:1：generic/摔落/溺水/岩浆/凶手 NPC）
    const cause = p.lastDamageCause ?? { kind: 'default' } as const;
    const deathText = createDeathText(this.playerName(), cause);
    // 原版 Player.cs:38312-38313:死亡消息走消息列,色 (225,25,25)
    this.newText(deathText, 225, 25, 25);
    // 红色粉碎粒子：身体范围多层爆散，喷溅落地反弹/停驻后缓慢淡出（约 5 秒）
    this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.y + 8, '#C02020', 22, 3.2, { life: 300, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
    this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.cy, '#8A1010', 16, 2.4, { life: 280, damp: 0.985, grav: 0.09 });
    this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.cy - 6, '#E85050', 10, 2.8, { life: 260, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
    // 墓碑（Player.DropTombstone 1:1）：按身家选款式，从死亡点抛射坠落，落格安放+碑文
    this.entities.add(new Tombstone(p.cx, p.cy, this.coinsOwned(), deathText, p.facing), 'props');
  }

  /** 身家（铜币计价）——商店/重铸扣费与墓碑金币门槛（>100000 → 金墓碑 527-531）。
   *  同时计 legacy coin_* 与原版 vi_71-74 两套钱币（wld 导入的钱币是后者） */
  coinsOwned(): number {
    const p = this.player;
    const n = (key: string, mult: number) => {
      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
      return id !== undefined ? p.inv.countOf(id) * mult : 0;
    };
    return n('coin_copper', 1) + n('coin_silver', 100) + n('coin_gold', 10000) + n('coin_platinum', 1000000)
      + n('vi_71_CopperCoin', 1) + n('vi_72_SilverCoin', 100) + n('vi_73_GoldCoin', 10000) + n('vi_74_PlatinumCoin', 1000000);
  }

  /** 角色名（死亡文本/墓碑碑文用；未命名回退） */
  playerName(): string {
    return this.player.appearance?.name?.trim() || Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.DefaultPlayerName');
  }


  respawnPlayer() {
    const p = this.player;
    p.hp = p.maxHp;
    p.dead = false;
    p.iframes = 90;
    p.respawnTimer = 0;
    p.immuneAlpha = 0;
    this.lostCoins = 0;
    p.x = this.world.spawnX * TILE + 8;
    p.y = (this.world.spawnY - 3) * TILE;
    p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0;
  }

  // ================= 渲染 =================

  render() {
    const cam = this.camera;
    const L = this.lighting;
    this.renderer.scene = this.scene; // 群系背景（BiomeBackground）每帧注入最新环境采样
    this.renderer.deathLostCoins = this.lostCoins; // 死亡 UI「掉了{0}」每帧注入
    this.renderer.annotateMarks = this.annotateMode ? this.marks : null;
    // 导线可见性:手持电路工具(mech)或 F7 强制(Main.cs:43548 DrawWires 开关语义)
    const held = this.player.inv.heldItem();
    this.renderer.showWires = this.forceShowWires || !!held && !!ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.wireTool;
    const hover = (this.input.uiBlocking || this.renderer.timeUiHover) ? null : (() => {
      // 智能光标激活：hover 指向策略选出的目标格（渲染层据此画原版黄框 + 方向箭头）
      const sc = this.smartCursor;
      if (sc.showing && this.world.store.inBounds(sc.x, sc.y)) {
        // 方向箭头显示门（Main.cs:46058-46065）：锁向下一格须 !active 或可替换块，
        // 否则传 null——渲染层见 null 即不画箭头（SmartCursor.smartCursorArrowAllowed）
        const arrow = sc.lockedDirection
          && smartCursorArrowAllowed(this.world.store, sc.x, sc.y, sc.lockedDirection)
          ? sc.lockedDirection : null;
        return { x: sc.x, y: sc.y, smart: true as const, arrow };
      }
      const [tx, ty] = cam.tileUnder(this.input.mouseX, this.input.mouseY);
      return this.world.store.inBounds(tx, ty) ? { x: tx, y: ty } : null;
    })();

      if (this.lastWasDay !== null) {
        this.newText(isDay ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.DayStart') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NightFall'), 50, 255, 130);

        this.triggerDayNightSensors();

      this.lastWasDay = isDay;

// 补齐清单打包批回归（事件月亮/进度条 wave20 在渲染层，此处覆盖可单测项）：
//  1. GenState.seedFlags 灌入（parseSeed → vanillaTerrain；金标种子旗标恒空）
//  2. 风筝 buff 383（KiteBuff：移速/镐速/kiteLuck 三档）+ 恶臭 buff 120（luck −0.25）
//  3. 银河珍珠 5340 / 漂亮镜子 5577 物品注册（vi_ 前缀图标管线可达）
//  4. 火把僵尸 590/591 手持火把点光（Enemy.torchZombieLight）
//  5. 吸血链（Arrow 命中 → applyLifeSteal 分发：304=vampire / magic=ghost）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { parseSeed } from '../src/world/gen/SeedEasterEggs';
import { newGenState } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GenState';
import { BuffState, BuffType } from '../src/stats/Buffs';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/items';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { Arrow } from '../src/entities/Arrow';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

describe('GenState.seedFlags 灌入（SeedEasterEggs.parseSeed）', () => {
  it('醉酒/十周年种子旗标解析；金标种子旗标恒空', () => {
    expect(parseSeed('drunk world').flags.drunkWorld).toBe(true);
    expect(parseSeed('celebrationmk10').flags.tenthAnniversary).toBe(true);
    expect(parseSeed('9293480').flags).toEqual({});
    expect(newGenState(100, 100).seedFlags).toEqual({});
  });
});

describe('风筝 buff 383 / 恶臭 buff 120（Player.cs:9612-9623 / :9958-9961）', () => {
  it('KiteBuff：移速 +0.25、镐速 ×0.85、kiteLuck 180s 一档钳 3', () => {
    const b = new BuffState();
    expect(b.kiteLuckLevel).toBe(0);
    b.apply(BuffType.KiteBuff, 179);
    expect(b.kiteLuckLevel).toBe(0);
    b.apply(BuffType.KiteBuff, 360);
    expect(b.kiteLuckLevel).toBe(2);
    b.apply(BuffType.KiteBuff, 10_000);
    expect(b.kiteLuckLevel).toBe(3);
    expect(b.pickSpeedMult).toBeCloseTo(0.85, 5);
    const noKite = new BuffState();
    expect(noKite.moveSpeedMult).toBe(1);
    b.remove(BuffType.KiteBuff);
    expect(b.moveSpeedMult).toBe(1);
    b.apply(BuffType.KiteBuff, 600);
    expect(b.moveSpeedMult).toBeCloseTo(1.25, 5);
  });

  it('luck 聚合：风筝档 +0.1/3·档、恶臭 −0.25（RecalculateLuck :28675/:28687-28690）', () => {
    const p = new Player(0, 0, new Inventory());
    p.buffs.apply(BuffType.KiteBuff, 360);
    expect(p.luck).toBeCloseTo((2 * 0.1) / 3, 5);
    p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Stinky, 60);
    expect(p.luck).toBeCloseTo((2 * 0.1) / 3 - 0.25, 5);
  });
});

describe('幸运消耗品/投掷物注册（5340 银河珍珠 / 5577 漂亮镜子）', () => {
  it('vi_5340 / vi_5577 均在物品表（vi_ 前缀图标管线可达）', () => {
    expect(ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_5340_galaxy_pearl']).toBeDefined();
    expect(ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_5577_pretty_mirror']).toBeDefined();
  });

  /** 弹幕砍 tileCut 物（Projectile.CutTiles 语义）：瓦罐→整罐碎裂，其余→breakTile */
  cutTile(x: number, y: number) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const t = st.get(x, y);
    if (t === POT_TILE) this.smashPot(x, y);
    else if (TILE_CUT.has(t)) this.breakTile(x, y);
  }

  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count = 1, vx = 0, vy = -2): ItemDrop | null {
    const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[itemKey];
    if (id === undefined) return null;
    const drop = new ItemDrop(x - 6, y - 6, id, count, vx, vy);
    this.entities.add(drop, 'drops');
    // 原版 Main.item 槽位上限 400,超出移除最旧掉落(Main.UpdateItems 空 slot 回收)——
    // 此前无上限:dev 模式一次 ~500 实体/雕像农场线性堆积(2026-08 审计 #10)
    const drops = this.entities.drops;
    while (drops.length > 400) drops.shift();
    return drop;
  }

  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: Enemy) {
    const p = this.player;
    if (p.dead) return;
    if (attacker) p.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'npc', name: (attacker.vanillaId != null ? Lang.npcName(attacker.vanillaId) : null) ?? attacker.def.name };
    const ok = p.damage(dmg, srcX, srcY);
    if (ok) {
      // 狼人(155)命中施加虚弱 33（1800t=30s：近战-5.1%伤/速、防-4、移-10%）
      if (attacker?.vanillaId === 155) p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Weak, 30);
      // 星星斗篷（Player.SpawnStar 近似）：受击召 3 颗落星砸附近敌人
      if (p.equipStats.starCloak) this.spawnStarCloakStars();
      // 蜂蜜斗篷/蜂巢背带（Player.SpawnBees :2147 近似）：受击放 1-3 只蜂（proj 181/183/189）
      if (p.equipStats.bees) {
        const n = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
        for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
          const tx = p.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 48;
          const ty = p.cy - 20 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 24;
          const tgt = this.entities.enemies.find((e) => {
            const en = e as Enemy;
            return !en.dead && Math.abs(en.cx - p.cx) < 500;
          }) as Enemy | undefined;
          const ang = tgt
            ? Math.atan2(tgt.cy - ty, tgt.cx - tx)
            : -Math.PI / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5);
          this.entities.add(new Arrow(tx, ty, Math.cos(ang) * 3.4, Math.sin(ang) * 3.4,
            Math.round(9 * p.damageMult('melee')), 0.5, 181, null), 'projectiles');
        }
        this.sfx.play('beeSummon');
      }
      // 致死一击：不播受击声，只播死亡声（保证最后听到的音效是死亡）
      if (p.hp <= 0) this.sfx.play('pkilled');
      else this.sfx.play('hurt');
      // 实际扣血 = max(1, dmg - defense*0.5)（在 Player.damage 内）
      const dealt = Math.max(1, dmg - p.defense * 0.5);
      this.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, Math.round(dealt), false, '#FF5050');
      // 荆棘 Buff（1.4.5.6 Player.cs:30910-30944）：thorns=1 → 反弹接触伤害全额
      // （num4 × thorns，上限 1000；num4=接触伤害原值，取受伤前的 dmg 而非扣血后 dealt）
      if (p.thornsActive && attacker && !attacker.dead) {
        const reflect = Math.min(1000, Math.round(dmg));
        attacker.hurt(reflect, Math.sign(attacker.cx - p.cx) || 1, -1.5, this);
        this.addDamageNumber(attacker.cx, attacker.y, reflect, false, '#80FF80');
      }
      if (p.hp <= 0) {
        p.dead = true;
        p.deathsPve++;              // numberOfDeathsPVE（PL:53840,存档持久化）
        p.respawnTimer = 0;
        // 死亡文本/墓碑由 handlePlayerDeath 统一处理（此处不再 toast「你死了」）
        // 掉钱按难度（Player.DropCoins :53351-53390：普通掉一半/专家 1/4/大师不掉；
        // 0=经典 1=专家 2=大师 3=旅程按经典）。垃圾桶死亡清空（Player.cs:53394）
        const diff = p.appearance?.difficulty ?? 0;
        const keep = diff === 2 ? 1 : diff === 1 ? 0.75 : 0.5;
        let lost = 0;
        const face = [1, 100, 10000, 1000000];
        const coinKeys = [
          ['coin_copper', 'vi_71_CopperCoin'], ['coin_silver', 'vi_72_SilverCoin'],
          ['coin_gold', 'vi_73_GoldCoin'], ['coin_platinum', 'vi_74_PlatinumCoin'],
        ];
        coinKeys.forEach(([ck, vk], ci) => {
          for (const k of [ck, vk]) {
            const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[k];
            if (id === undefined) continue;
            const n = p.inv.countOf(id);
            if (n > 0) {
              lost += (n - Math.floor(n * keep)) * face[ci];
              p.inv.remove(id, n - Math.floor(n * keep));
            }
          }
        });
        this.lostCoins = lost; // DrawInterface_35 "掉了{0}"（重生于 respawnPlayer 清零）
        p.inv.trash = null;
      }
    }

5	import { validateAppearance, type Appearance } from '../player/Appearance';

7	const INDEX_KEY = 'sandboxworld.players.index';
8	const dataKey = (id: number) => `sandboxworld.player.${id}`;
9	
10	export interface CharacterMeta { id: number; name: string; difficulty: number; createdAt: number; }
11	
12	let nextId = 1;
13	
14	export class CharacterStore {
15	  private index: CharacterMeta[] = [];
16	  private loaded = false;
17	
18	  async ensureLoaded() {
19	    if (this.loaded) return;
20	    const raw = await kvGet(INDEX_KEY);
21	    if (raw) {
22	      try {
23	        const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
24	        if (Array.isArray(arr)) this.index = arr.filter((x) => x && typeof x.id === 'number');

26	    }

30

32	    await kvSet(INDEX_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.index));
33	  }

36

38	    if (!await kvHas(dataKey(id))) return null;

45	    }
46	  }

51	    this.index.push(meta);
52	    await this.saveIndex();
53	    await kvSet(dataKey(meta.id), JSON.stringify(a));
54	    return meta;
55	  }
56	
57	  async update(id: number, a: Appearance) {
58	    await this.ensureLoaded();

60	    if (!m) return;
61	    m.name = a.name || m.name;

65	  }
66	
67	  async delete(id: number) {

72	  }

41	export function defaultAppearance(name = ''): Appearance {
42	  return {
43	    name,
44	    hair: 0,               // 0 基（贴图 Player_Hair_1.png）
45	    skinVariant: 0,
46	    ...structuredClone(DEFAULT_COLORS),
47	    difficulty: 0,
48	  };
49	}
50	
51	/** 旧数据/损坏数据补默认；颜色钳到 0-255 */
52	export function validateAppearance(a: Partial<Appearance> | null | undefined): Appearance {
53	  const d = defaultAppearance();
54	  if (!a) return d;
55	  const c = (v: unknown, fallback: RGBColor): RGBColor => {
56	    if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null) return fallback;
57	    const o = v as Partial<RGBColor>;
58	    const cl = (n: unknown) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(Number(n) || 0)));
59	    return { r: cl(o.r), g: cl(o.g), b: cl(o.b) };
60	  };
61	  return {
62	    name: typeof a.name === 'string' ? a.name.slice(0, 20) : d.name,
63	    // 1.4.5.6 保护（Player.cs:54209）：hair >= 228 → 0
64	    hair: clampInt(a.hair, 0, HAIR_COUNT - 1, d.hair),
65	    skinVariant: clampInt(a.skinVariant, 0, 11, d.skinVariant),
66	    hairColor: c(a.hairColor, d.hairColor),
67	    skinColor: c(a.skinColor, d.skinColor),
68	    eyeColor: c(a.eyeColor, d.eyeColor),
69	    shirtColor: c(a.shirtColor, d.shirtColor),
70	    undershirtColor: c(a.undershirtColor, d.undershirtColor),
71	    pantsColor: c(a.pantsColor, d.pantsColor),
72	    shoeColor: c(a.shoeColor, d.shoeColor),
73	    difficulty: ([0, 1, 2, 3] as number[]).includes(a.difficulty as number) ? (a.difficulty as Difficulty) : 0,
74	  };
75	}
76	
77	/** UICharacterCreation.cs:58 的有效衣服样式（1.4.5.6：前 5 男装 / 后 5 女装） */
78	export const VALID_CLOTH_STYLES = [0, 2, 1, 3, 8, 9, 7, 5, 6, 4];
79	
80	/** PlayerVariantID.Sets.Male / VariantOrderFemale（1.4.5.6） */
81	export const MALE_VARIANTS = [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 10];
82	export const FEMALE_VARIANTS = [4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 11];
83	
84	/** 发型 id 为 0 基（0..227）；贴图 = Player_Hair_{id+1}.png（AssetInitializer:455） */
85	export const HAIR_COUNT = 228;
86	
87	/** 角色创建可用发型白名单（HairstyleUnlocksHelper.RebuildList：0-50 + 136-144,147-151,154,155,157,158,161
88	 *  + 51-122 + 134,135,146,152,153,156,159,165,160 + 166-227） */
89	export const CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS: number[] = (() => {
90	  const list: number[] = [];
91	  for (let i = 0; i <= 50; i++) list.push(i);
92	  list.push(136, 137, 138, 139, 140, 141, 142, 143, 144, 147, 148, 149, 150, 151, 154, 155, 157, 158, 161);
93	  for (let i = 51; i <= 122; i++) list.push(i);
94	  list.push(134, 135, 146, 152, 153, 156, 159, 165, 160);
95	  for (let i = 166; i < HAIR_COUNT; i++) list.push(i);
96	  return list;
97	})();
98	
99	/** 女性发型表（UICharacterCreation 随机逻辑的 switch(hair+1)，case-1 还原为 0 基 id） */
100	export const FEMALE_HAIRS = new Set([4, 5, 6, 9, 11, 18, 21, 22, 25, 26, 29, 32, 33, 34, 36, 37, 38, 39, 40,
101	  43, 44, 45, 46, 47, 48, 50, 55, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 70, 71, 72, 73, 78, 79, 80, 81, 83, 84, 85, 86, 87,
102	  89, 90, 91, 92, 94, 95, 97, 99, 101, 103, 106, 107, 112, 123, 125, 132, 133, 134, 143, 145, 146, 162, 164]);
103	
104	/** 后发层判定（Player.GetHairSettings:16771，0 基 id） */
105	export function backHairDraw(hair: number): boolean {
106	  if (hair === 133 || hair === 134 || hair === 146 || hair === 162 || hair === 6) return true;
107	  return hair > 50 && (hair < 56 || hair > 63) && (hair < 74 || hair > 77) && (hair < 88 || hair > 89)
108	    && hair !== 94 && hair !== 100 && hair !== 104 && hair !== 112 && hair < 116;
109	}
110	
111	/** 随机外观（对齐 1.4.5.6 CreateCharacter：从白名单随机发型，
112	 *  性别由发型判定表推导（FEMALE_HAIRS），变体从对应性别列表取） */
113	export function randomAppearance(name = ''): Appearance {
114	  const a = defaultAppearance(name);
115	  a.hair = CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CHAR_CREATION_HAIRS.length)];
116	  const pool = FEMALE_HAIRS.has(a.hair) ? FEMALE_VARIANTS : MALE_VARIANTS;
117	  a.skinVariant = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
118	  const hsl = (h: number, s: number, l: number) => {
119	    const [r, g, b] = hslToRgb(h / 360, s, l);
120	    return { r: Math.round(r * 255), g: Math.round(g * 255), b: Math.round(b * 255) };
121	  };
122	  a.skinColor = hsl(20 + Math.random() * 20, 0.45, 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.25);
123	  a.hairColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.5, 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.4);
124	  a.eyeColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.3);
125	  a.shirtColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.2);
126	  a.undershirtColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.2);
127	  a.pantsColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.35, 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.3);
128	  a.shoeColor = hsl(Math.random() * 360, 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4, 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.25);
129	  a.difficulty = 0;
130	  return a;
131	}
132	
133	function clampInt(v: unknown, min: number, max: number, fallback: number): number {
134	  const n = Math.round(Number(v));
135	  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return fallback;
136	  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
137	}
138	
139	/** HSL → RGB（0-1 输入/输出） */
140	export function hslToRgb(h: number, s: number, l: number): [number, number, number] {
141	  h = ((h % 1) + 1) % 1;
142	  const f = (n: number) => {
143	    const k = (n + h * 12) % 12;
144	    return l - s * Math.min(l, 1 - l) * Math.max(-1, Math.min(k - 3, 9 - k, 1));
145	  };
146	  return [f(0), f(8), f(4)];
147	}
148	
149	export function rgbToHsl(r: number, g: number, b: number): [number, number, number] {
150	  const rr = r / 255, gg = g / 255, bb = b / 255;
151	  const max = Math.max(rr, gg, bb), min = Math.min(rr, gg, bb);
152	  const l = (max + min) / 2;
153	  let h = 0, s = 0;
154	  if (max !== min) {
155	    const d = max - min;
156	    s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
157	    if (max === rr) h = ((gg - bb) / d + (gg < bb ? 6 : 0)) / 6;
158	    else if (max === gg) h = ((bb - rr) / d + 2) / 6;
159	    else h = ((rr - gg) / d + 4) / 6;
160	  }
161	  return [h, s, l];
162	}
163	

```

138	      mk(Lang.text('UI.Delete'), false, async () => {

140	        await this.store.delete(meta.id);

145	      const enter = () => this.close(() => this.flow.onPlay(a));
146	      el.addEventListener('click', enter);

148	      this.body.appendChild(el);
149	      this.items.push({ meta, appearance, canvas });

169	    }

171	  };

  });

describe('吸血链分发（Projectile.cs:12877-12892）', () => {
  interface Log { calls: Array<[string, number]>; }
  function makeGame(log: Log, enemy: unknown): GameHooks {
    return {
      world: new World(64, 32, 42, '测试'),
      player: new Player(0, 0, new Inventory()),
      enemies: () => [enemy], critters: () => [], spawnDrop: () => null,
      damagePlayer: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},
      spawnParticles: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {},
      applyLifeSteal: (kind: 'vampire' | 'ghost', dmg: number) => { log.calls.push([kind, dmg]); return 0; },
    } as unknown as GameHooks;
  }
  function targetEnemy(x: number): { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: () => boolean } {
    return { x, y: 16 * 16, w: 16, h: 16, id: 1, dead: false, hurt: () => true };
  }

  it('吸血鬼飞刀 304 命中 → vampire（系数 0.075 侧）', () => {
    const log: Log = { calls: [] };
    const game = makeGame(log, targetEnemy(30 * 16));
    const a = new Arrow(30 * 16 - 30, 16 * 16 + 8, 6, 0, 100, 0, 304, null, { grav: 0, life: 300, pierce: 1 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 8 && !a.dead; i++) a.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);
    expect(log.calls.length).toBe(1);
    expect(log.calls[0][0]).toBe('vampire');
    expect(log.calls[0][1]).toBe(100);
  });

  it('魔法弹命中 → ghost；非魔法非 304 弹不分发', () => {
    const log: Log = { calls: [] };
    const game = makeGame(log, targetEnemy(30 * 16));
    // 恶魔镰刀 15（vanilla-projectiles magic=true）
    const a = new Arrow(30 * 16 - 30, 16 * 16 + 8, 6, 0, 60, 0, 15, null, { grav: 0, life: 300, pierce: 1 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 8 && !a.dead; i++) a.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);
    expect(log.calls.length).toBe(1);
    expect(log.calls[0][0]).toBe('ghost');
    const log2: Log = { calls: [] };
    const game2 = makeGame(log2, targetEnemy(30 * 16));
    // 木箭 1（melee/ranged 均非）→ 不进吸血链
    const b = new Arrow(30 * 16 - 30, 16 * 16 + 8, 6, 0, 60, 0, 1, null, { grav: 0, life: 300, pierce: 1 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 8 && !b.dead; i++) b.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game2);
    expect(log2.calls.length).toBe(0);
  });
});

// 掉落系统回归（对照 1.4.5.6 ItemDropDatabase 提取表 + NPC 掉落管线）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import {
  resolveDrops, rollCoins, rollHeartsAndStars, npcValueOf,
  type NpcDropCtx,
} from '../src/drops/NpcDrops';
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID } from '../src/data/items';
import dropData from '../src/data/vanilla-npcdrops.json';

function mkCtx(over: Partial<NpcDropCtx> = {}): NpcDropCtx {
  return {
    rng: new RNG(42),
    netId: 1, type: 1,
    isExpert: false, isMaster: false, hardMode: false, crimson: false,
    flags: {},
    x: 1000, y: 1000,
    worldSurfaceTile: 200, rockLayerTile: 400, underworldTileY: 900,
    maxTilesX: 4200, maxTilesY: 1200,
    lifeMax: 50, friendly: false, boss: false, damage: 10, value: 10,
    hp: 80, hpMax: 100, mana: 15, manaMax: 20,
    ...over,
  };
}

/** 采样 N 次击杀：每杀独立 rng，返回 id→总件数 与 每杀明细（互斥断言用） */
function sample(type: number, n: number, over: Partial<NpcDropCtx> = {}) {
  const counts = new Map<number, number>();
  const perKill: Array<number[]> = [];
  let zeroValue = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const c = mkCtx({ rng: new RNG(i * 7919 + 13), netId: type, type, ...over });
    const res = resolveDrops(c);
    zeroValue = zeroValue || res.zeroValue;
    const ids: number[] = [];
    for (const d of res.drops) {
      counts.set(d.id, (counts.get(d.id) ?? 0) + d.n);
      ids.push(d.id);
    }
    perKill.push(ids);
  }
  return { counts, perKill, zeroValue };
}

describe('掉落提取表（静态）', () => {

    const npcs = new Set<number>();
    const kinds = new Set<string>();
    for (const e of entries as Array<{ npcs?: number[]; rule: { k: string } }>) {
      for (const n of e.npcs ?? []) npcs.add(n);
      kinds.add(e.rule.k);
    }
    expect(npcs.size).toBeGreaterThan(380);
    expect(kinds.has('unknown')).toBe(false);
  });

  async newWorld(seedText: string, width: number, height: number, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void, opts?: { name?: string; evil?: -1 | 0 | 1; onWorldPartial?: (world: import('../world/World').World, passIndex: number, passName: string) => void; /** worker 路径的降采样预览帧回调（位图模式） */ onPreview?: (f: import('../workers/WorldGenClient').PreviewFrame) => void }) {
    this.freshlyCreated = true; // NPC 入驻公告只在新生成的世界播(WorldGen 语义)
    const settleLabel = () => Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.SettleLiquids');
    // ---- worker 路径（generate + settle 一条链在后台完成，UI 全程不卡） ----
    if (!this.genClient) this.genClient = new WorldGenClient();
    if (await this.genClient.probe()) {
      try {
        this.world = await this.genClient.generate(
          { width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, preview: !!opts?.onPreview },
          {
            onPreview: opts?.onPreview,
            // 进度区间映射与主线程路径一致：generate 0–0.7、settle 0.72–0.87
            onProgress: (phase, label, p) => {
              if (phase === 'generate') onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7);
              else onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72 + p * 0.15);
            },
          },
        );
        await this.preloadSceneAssets((label, p) => onProgress?.(label, 0.87 + p * 0.13));
        onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.Done'), 1);
        this.afterWorldLoad();
        this.cb.onWorldReady();
        return;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e; // 真实业务错误（如 OOM）不吞
        // worker 失败 → 落回主线程路径
      }
    }
    // ---- 主线程 fallback（原路径原样保留：worker 不可用 + 探针依赖） ----
    this.world = await generateWorld({ width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, onWorldPartial: opts?.onWorldPartial }, (label, p) => onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7));
    // 水体预沉降：创建世界时全图激活+步进至稳定，带进度条
    onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72);
    await this.settleLiquids((p) => onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72 + p * 0.15));
    // 进图前贴图预载(用户要求:不进图后才动态加载)
    await this.preloadSceneAssets((label, p) => onProgress?.(label, 0.87 + p * 0.13));
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.Done'), 1);
    this.afterWorldLoad();
    this.cb.onWorldReady();
  }

  /** 进图前统一预载:onWorldReady 之前把首帧画面涉及的贴图全部就位。
   *  图块/墙表按【出生点区域类型扫描】精确预载(半径 240 实测仅 22/378 张表,
   *  而非全量 ~250MB)——远行遇到的类型走懒加载,onVImageLoaded 回调全量标脏
   *  chunk 自动重烘焙;物品图标全量(18MB);角色 = Player_ 全量 + 当前装备的
   *  3 张 Armor 表(换装走懒加载);出生点森林背景 */
  private async preloadSceneAssets(onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
    const a = this.atlas;
    if (!a) return;
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadWorldTex'), 0.05);
    // 出生点半径类型扫描:只预载画面涉及的图块/墙表
    const st = this.world.store;
    const { tileSheets, wallIds } = this.collectSheetsAround(this.world.spawnX, this.world.spawnY, 240);
    await Promise.all([
      a.preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets, wallIds),
      a.preloadMiscAndNpcs(),
    ]);
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadItemIcons'), 0.15);
    // 物品图标（6059 张/24MB）改为后台补齐——不再阻塞进图：渲染/道具栏走
    // vicon→ensureVImage 懒加载（未就绪回退程序化图标，paintSlot 每次刷新自愈升级）；
    // 补齐完成后触发一次背包刷新，把兜底图标原地替换为原版图标
    void a.preloadIcons().then(() => this.cb.onInventoryChanged?.());
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadCharTex'), 0.7);
    // Player_ 全量(纸娃娃全部身体通道) + 仅当前装备的 Armor 表;
    // 换装时 vui 懒加载 + PaperDoll 就绪预检自动兜住
    const armorFiles = new Set<string>();
    for (const id of this.playerPreviewArmor()) {
      if (id == null) continue;
      const def = ITEM_DEFS[id];
      if (!def) continue;
      const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[def.key] ?? (def.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(def.key.slice(3), 10) : NaN);
      const entry = Number.isFinite(vid) ? a.vanilla.armorIndex?.[String(vid)] : null;
      if (!entry) continue;
      if (entry.head) armorFiles.add(`Armor_Head_${entry.head}.png`);
      if (entry.body) armorFiles.add(`Armor_Armor_${entry.body}.png`);
      if (entry.legs) armorFiles.add(`Armor_Legs_${entry.legs}.png`);
    }
    await a.preloadUiPrefix(['Player_']);
    await a.preloadUiFiles(armorFiles);
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadBg'), 0.9);
    await this.renderer.biomeBg.preloadInitial(this.world);
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadBg'), 1);
  }

  /** 区域类型扫描:中心 ±R 内出现的图块 sheet id + 墙 id 集合(出生点预载/传送门共用) */
  private collectSheetsAround(cx: number, cy: number, R: number): { tileSheets: Set<number>; wallIds: Set<number> } {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const tileSheets = new Set<number>();
    const wallIds = new Set<number>();
    for (let x = Math.max(0, cx - R); x < Math.min(st.w, cx + R); x++) {
      for (let y = Math.max(0, cy - R); y < Math.min(st.h, cy + R); y++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        if (st.flags[i]) {
          const vs = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]]?.vanilla?.sheet;
          if (vs !== undefined) tileSheets.add(vs);
        }
        if (st.wall[i] > 0) wallIds.add(st.wall[i]);
      }
    }
    // 兜底常驻:草/泥/石表全时有(暴露地表转草、autotile 邻接)
    for (const k of ['dirt', 'stone', 'grass']) {
      const vs = TILE_DEFS[TILE_BY_KEY[k]]?.vanilla?.sheet;
      if (vs !== undefined) tileSheets.add(vs);
    }
    return { tileSheets, wallIds };
  }

  /** 预载期的装备槽位(此时 player 尚未创建,读初始装备常量:afterWorldLoad 同源) */
  private playerPreviewArmor(): Array<number | null> {
    // 与 afterWorldLoad 初始装备一致:铁三件(头/胸/腿)
    const ids: Array<number | null> = [null, null, null];
    const order = ['iron_helmet', 'iron_chainmail', 'iron_greaves'];
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[order[k]];
      ids[k] = id !== undefined ? id : null;
    }
    return ids;
  }

  /** 全图液体沉降（薄封装 → settleWorldLiquids，主线程 fallback 与 worker 共用同一实现）。
   *  mode 语义见 src/world/liquid/settle.ts 头注释 */
  private settleLiquids(onProgress?: (p: number) => void, mode: 'gen' | 'load' = 'gen') {
    return settleWorldLiquids(this.world, mode, onProgress);
  }

  async loadWorld(world: World, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void, opts?: { settled?: boolean }) {
    this.freshlyCreated = false;
    this.world = world;
    if (!opts?.settled) {
      onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.SettleLiquids'), 0.5);
      // 读档模式：原版 WorldFile 单轮语义（存档水体已静止，QuickWater+WaterCheck 后
      // 活水极少，单轮快速收敛；残留交给运行时——不再跑生成期的 10 轮全沉降）

}

/** 玩家储物族 tile key → bank 索引（原版 Player.cs:32598+：29→bank / 97→bank2 /
 *  463→bank3 / 491→bank4，chest 槽位约定 -2..-5） */
const BANK_TILES: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, number]> = [
  ['v_29_piggy_bank', 0], ['v_97_safe', 1], ['v_463_defenders_forge', 2], ['v_491_void_vault', 3],
];

/** 就地剔除 life<=0 的粒子/飘字(保序零分配,2026-08 审计 G9) */
function compactByLife<T extends { life: number }>(list: T[]): void {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
    if (list[r].life > 0) list[w++] = list[r];
  }
  list.length = w;
}

export interface GameCallbacks {
  onWorldReady: () => void;
  onInventoryChanged: () => void;
  onToast: (msg: string) => void;
  /** 原版 Main.NewText 消息列(Main.cs:64095 → LegacyChatMonitor;RGB 0-255) */
  /** width = NewTextMultiline WidthLimit（-1 不限；广播盒 460） */
  onChat?: (text: string, r: number, g: number, b: number, width?: number) => void;
  /** NPC 对话框(SetTalkNPC):name/chat/buttons → UI 渲染 */
  onNpcDialog?: (name: string, chat: string, buttons: Array<{ id: 'shop' | 'heal' | 'curse' | 'collect' | 'reforge' | 'close'; label: string }>, portrait?: string | null) => void;
  onNpcDialogClose?: () => void;
  /** 哥布林重铸界面（Main.InReforgeMenu） */
  onReforgeOpen?: () => void;
  /** 商店面板(SetupShop):条目(图标由 UI 按原版 id 补)+ 当前铜币 */
  onNpcShop?: (title: string, items: Array<{ key: string; vanillaId: number; name: string; price: number }>, copper: number) => void;
  onBuffsChanged?: () => void;
  /** 读墓碑/告示牌（Sign 阅读界面） */
  onReadSign?: (text: string) => void;
  /** 广播盒右键（Player.cs:32231-32250 OpenSign）：开文本编辑面板（锚点+现文本） */
  onEditSign?: (x: number, y: number, text: string) => void;
  onDayNight?: (isDay: boolean) => void;
  /** 环境选曲变化（MusicID；0=静音）。原版 UpdateAudio_DecideOnNewMusic 驱动 */
  onMusic?: (musicId: number) => void;
}

/** Main.time 口径（Main.cs 昼夜计时）：黎明 4:30（timeOfDay 0.25）归零，
 *  白天 54000 ticks、夜晚 32400 ticks。WorldGen.IsAlchemyPlantHarvestable（:66214）用。 */
function vanillaMainTime(timeOfDay: number): number {
  // timeOfDay↔Main.time：tick 恒速 86400t/周期 → 昼 (t-DAWN)*86400 / 夜 (t-DUSK mod 1)*86400
  if (timeOfDay >= Clock.DAWN && timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK) return (timeOfDay - Clock.DAWN) * 86400;
  return (((timeOfDay - Clock.DUSK) % 1) + 1) % 1 * 86400;
}

export class Game implements GameHooks {
  assets: AssetBundle;
  atlas: SpriteAtlas | null = null;
  autotiler: AutoTiler | null = null;
  world!: World;
  player!: Player;
  camera!: Camera;
  renderer: Renderer;
  chunks!: ChunkCache;
  lighting!: LightingEngine;
  liquid!: LiquidSim;
  /** 世界运行时演化（WorldGen.UpdateWorld 移植）：草蔓延/藤蔓/药草/邪恶扩散 */
  evolution!: WorldEvolution;
  /** Torch God 彩蛋事件（Player.cs:17585-17770）：>100 根点燃火把地下触发 */
  torchGod!: TorchGodEvent;
  /** 黑暗视野（buff 80 Blackout）：updateLightDecay ×0.85 */
  blackout = false;
  entities = new EntityManager();
  input: Input;
  cb: GameCallbacks;
  sfx = new Sfx();

```

933:    header: {
  const tileNames = vanillaTileNames;
  const itemNames = vanillaItemNames;
  const deg = new Map<number, number>(), clr = new Map<number, number>(), itm = new Map<number, number>();
  const bump = (m: Map<number, number>, id: number) => m.set(id, (m.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);

  const w = wld.tilesWide, h = wld.tilesHigh;
  const n = w * h;
  const type = new Uint16Array(n);
  const frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
  const frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
  const wall = new Uint16Array(n); // 原版墙 id 直存（0-366）
  const liquidArr = new Uint8Array(n);
  const liquidTypeArr = new Uint8Array(n);
  const wireArr = new Uint8Array(n);
  const halfArr = new Uint8Array(n);
  const slopeArr = new Uint8Array(n);

  const chestAt = new Map<string, { x: number; y: number }>();
  for (const c of wld.chests) chestAt.set(`${c.x},${c.y}`, c);

  let unmapped = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const wt = wld.tiles.type[i];
    if (wld.tiles.active[i]) {
      const mapped = TILE_MAP[wt];
      if (mapped === undefined) {
        // 未显式登记 → sheet 兜底（全量补齐后 753 sheet 全覆盖，正常不再走到降级）
        const fb = sheetFallback(wt);
        if (fb) {
          type[i] = fb.id;
          if (fb.copyFrame) { frameX[i] = wld.tiles.frameX[i]; frameY[i] = wld.tiles.frameY[i]; }
        } else {
          unmapped++;
          bump(deg, wt);
          type[i] = T.STONE;
        }
      } else if (mapped === null) {
        bump(clr, wt);
        type[i] = 0;
      } else if (typeof mapped === 'string') {
        type[i] = TILE_BY_KEY[mapped] ?? 0;
        // ★framed 图块(铁砧/工作台/火把/v_ 家具)同样要直拷原版帧——
        // 纯字符串映射此前不拷帧,2×1 铁砧右半格 (18,0) 丢失,每格都画左半边
        // ("只有一半的铁砧");火把墙挂变体/家具 style 帧也全丢。非 framed 方块帧无意义
        if (TILE_DEFS[type[i]]?.framed) {
          frameX[i] = wld.tiles.frameX[i];
          frameY[i] = wld.tiles.frameY[i];
        }
      } else {
        // 原版 framed 家具：18px 帧/样式偏移直拷（与 .wld 原生一致，渲染端零换算）
        type[i] = TILE_BY_KEY[mapped.key] ?? 0;

// worldGen worker ↔ 主线程协议：世界数据包（packWorld 打包 / World.fromPacket 重建）
// + 生成/读档请求与事件联合。（type-only 模块——运行时消息按此形状对齐）
import type { SaveData } from '../save/serialize';
import type { ChestData } from '../world/World';
import type { WeatherSave } from '../world/weather/Weather';
import type { BestiarySave } from '../data/Bestiary';
import type { FurnitureItemRecord } from '../world/FurnitureItems';

/** tile 存储的转移所有权 buffer 组（worker postMessage transfer 零拷贝） */
export interface TileBuffers {
  type: ArrayBuffer; flags: ArrayBuffer;
  frameX: ArrayBuffer; frameY: ArrayBuffer;
  wall: ArrayBuffer; liquid: ArrayBuffer; liquidType: ArrayBuffer;
  half: ArrayBuffer; slope: ArrayBuffer; wire: ArrayBuffer;
  /** 战争迷雾（旧包可缺省——fromPacket 以全零补齐；saveGame 不持久化 explored） */
  explored?: ArrayBuffer;
}

export interface WorldPacket {
  w: number; h: number; seed: number; name: string;
  spawnX: number; spawnY: number;
  groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine: number;
  crimson: boolean; dungeonX: number; dungeonY: number; jungleX: number;
  exploredVersion: number;
  clock: { timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number; bloodMoon?: boolean; eclipse?: boolean; moonPhase?: number };
  /** 月亮贴图样式（原版 header moonType；旧包缺省 0） */
  moonType?: number;
  /** 入侵五元组（生成路径恒 0；读档路径经本包回传——packWorld 曾漏传致 worker 读档丢入侵） */
  invasion?: { type: number; size: number; sizeStart: number; x: number; delay: number };
  /** 已砸祭坛数（读档路径回传;旧包缺省 0） */
  altarCount?: number;
  /** 彩蛋种子旗标（SeedEasterEggs.parseSeed；血月 1/6 十周年 / drunkWorld 黎明翻转；旧包缺省={}） */
  seedFlags?: import('../world/gen/SeedEasterEggs').SeedFlags;
  /** 天气快照（读档路径回传;旧包缺省=晴） */
  weather?: WeatherSave;
  /** 图鉴三桶（读档路径回传;旧包缺省=空） */
  bestiary?: BestiarySave;
  /** 城镇 NPC 段（读档路径回传,Game.loadWorld 消费;旧包缺省=空→新世界出生分支） */
  pendingTownNpcs?: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null; bound?: boolean; givenName?: string }>;
  chests: ChestData[];
  signs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; text: string }>;
  /** 挂物族存放物（物品框/武器架/模特/帽架/食物盘/展示罐；生成路径无、读档路径有） */
  furnitureItems?: FurnitureItemRecord[];
  trees: Array<{ x: number; y: number; h: number }>;
  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
  treeX: number[]; treeStyle: number[]; treeTops: number[];
  /** 以太生物群系中心（无微光世界为 0；旧包可缺省） */
  shimmerX?: number; shimmerY?: number;
  buf: TileBuffers;
}

/** 生成配置（主线程 → worker；GenClient.generate 入参直传） */
export interface GenConfigDTO {
  width: number; height: number;
  seedText: string;
  name?: string;
  evil?: 0 | 1 | -1;  // 0 腐化 / 1 猩红 / -1 随机
  preview: boolean;   // 生成期降采样位图预览开关
}

----

          // 生成期实时预览：worker 侧降采样 RGBA → transfer（替代旧 onWorldPartial 整 world 回调）

              const f = renderPreviewBitmap(w.store);
              ctx.postMessage(
                { id: req.id, type: 'preview', passIndex, passName, width: f.width, height: f.height, rgba: f.rgba.buffer as ArrayBuffer },
                [f.rgba.buffer],
              );
            }

        },

      );
      await settleWorldLiquids(world, 'gen',
        (p) => ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'progress', phase: 'settle', label: '水体沉降', p }));
      const { packet, transfer } = packWorld(world); // 转移所有权（本分支最后一步）
      ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'result', world: packet }, transfer);
      return;
    }
    if (req.type === 'saveParse') {
      // 三源：key=worker 内直读 IDB（主路径，免大字符串 clone）；json=小档/调试；save=.wld 导入
      const data = req.save
        ? req.save
        : JSON.parse(req.json ?? (await kvGetIdb(req.key ?? '')) ?? 'null') as import('../save/SaveFile').SaveData | null;
      if (!data) throw new Error('存档数据缺失');
      ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'progress', phase: 'parse', p: 0.3 });
      const { world, player } = loadSaveData(data);
      await settleWorldLiquids(world, 'load',
        (p) => ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'progress', phase: 'settle', label: '水体沉降', p: 0.3 + p * 0.5 }));
      const { packet, transfer } = packWorld(world);
      ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'result', world: packet, player, save: data }, transfer);
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    ctx.postMessage({
      id: req.id, type: 'error',
      message: err instanceof Error ? `${err.message}` : String(err),
      stack: err instanceof Error ? err.stack : undefined,
    });
  }

    const settleLabel = () => Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.SettleLiquids');
    // 世界难度（Main.GameMode，Main.cs:2677）：worker/主线程两路生成完成后统一灌入——
    // worker GenConfig 不带此字段，fromPacket 回 0，此处覆盖（创建 UI 已选档）
    const applyDifficulty = () => { if (opts?.difficulty !== undefined) this.world.difficulty = opts.difficulty; };
    // ---- worker 路径（generate + settle 一条链在后台完成，UI 全程不卡） ----
    if (!this.genClient) this.genClient = new WorldGenClient();
    if (await this.genClient.probe()) {
      try {
        this.world = await this.genClient.generate(
          { width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, preview: !!opts?.onPreview },
          {
            onPreview: opts?.onPreview,
            // 进度区间映射与主线程路径一致：generate 0–0.7、settle 0.72–0.87
            onProgress: (phase, label, p) => {
              if (phase === 'generate') onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7);
              else onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72 + p * 0.15);
            },
          },
        );
        await this.preloadSceneAssets((label, p) => onProgress?.(label, 0.87 + p * 0.13));
        onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.Done'), 1);
        this.afterWorldLoad();
        this.cb.onWorldReady();
        return;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e; // 真实业务错误（如 OOM）不吞
        // worker 失败 → 落回主线程路径
      }
    }
    // ---- 主线程 fallback（原路径原样保留：worker 不可用 + 探针依赖） ----
    this.world = await generateWorld({ width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, onWorldPartial: opts?.onWorldPartial }, (label, p) => onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7));
    // 水体预沉降：创建世界时全图激活+步进至稳定，带进度条
    onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72);
    await this.settleLiquids((p) => onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72 + p * 0.15));
    // 进图前贴图预载(用户要求:不进图后才动态加载)
    await this.preloadSceneAssets((label, p) => onProgress?.(label, 0.87 + p * 0.13));
    onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.Done'), 1);
    this.afterWorldLoad();
    this.cb.onWorldReady();

    // 死亡文本（Lang.CreateDeathMessage 1:1：generic/摔落/溺水/岩浆/凶手 NPC）
    const cause = p.lastDamageCause ?? { kind: 'default' } as const;
    const deathText = createDeathText(this.playerName(), cause);
    // 原版 Player.cs:38312-38313:死亡消息走消息列,色 (225,25,25)
    this.newText(deathText, 225, 25, 25);
    // 红色粉碎粒子：身体范围多层爆散，喷溅落地反弹/停驻后缓慢淡出（约 5 秒）
    this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.y + 8, '#C02020', 22, 3.2, { life: 300, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
    this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.cy, '#8A1010', 16, 2.4, { life: 280, damp: 0.985, grav: 0.09 });
    this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.cy - 6, '#E85050', 10, 2.8, { life: 260, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
    // 墓碑（Player.DropTombstone 1:1）：按身家选款式，从死亡点抛射坠落，落格安放+碑文
    this.entities.add(new Tombstone(p.cx, p.cy, this.coinsOwned(), deathText, p.facing), 'props');
  }

  /** 身家（铜币计价）——商店/重铸扣费与墓碑金币门槛（>100000 → 金墓碑 527-531）。
   *  同时计 legacy coin_* 与原版 vi_71-74 两套钱币（wld 导入的钱币是后者） */
  coinsOwned(): number {
    const p = this.player;
    const n = (key: string, mult: number) => {
      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
      return id !== undefined ? p.inv.countOf(id) * mult : 0;
    };
    return n('coin_copper', 1) + n('coin_silver', 100) + n('coin_gold', 10000) + n('coin_platinum', 1000000)
      + n('vi_71_CopperCoin', 1) + n('vi_72_SilverCoin', 100) + n('vi_73_GoldCoin', 10000) + n('vi_74_PlatinumCoin', 1000000);
  }

  /** 角色名（死亡文本/墓碑碑文用；未命名回退） */
  playerName(): string {
    return this.player.appearance?.name?.trim() || Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.DefaultPlayerName');
  }


  respawnPlayer() {
    const p = this.player;
    p.hp = p.maxHp;
    p.dead = false;
    p.iframes = 90;
    p.respawnTimer = 0;
    p.immuneAlpha = 0;
    this.lostCoins = 0;
    p.x = this.world.spawnX * TILE + 8;
    p.y = (this.world.spawnY - 3) * TILE;
    p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0;
  }

  // ================= 渲染 =================

ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.key;\n        if (!key) continue;\n        this.spawnDrop(p.cx, p.cy, key, s.stack, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8, -2 - Math.random() * 2);\n      }\n    };\n    drop(p.inv.slots);\n    drop(p.inv.armor);\n    drop(p.inv.dye);\n    drop(p.inv.miscEquips);\n    drop(p.inv.miscDyes);\n    for (const l of p.inv.loadouts) { drop(l.armor); drop(l.dye); }  // EquipmentLoadout.TryDroppingItems\n    p.inv.slots.fill(null);\n    p.inv.armor.fill(null);\n    p.inv.dye.fill(null);\n    p.inv.miscEquips.fill(null);\n    p.inv.miscDyes.fill(null);\n    for (const l of p.inv.loadouts) { l.armor.fill(null); l.dye.fill(null); }\n    // 发还铜制起始三件套（原版 1:1 顺序：[0]短剑 3507 / [1]镐 3509 / [2]斧 3506）\n    const starter = [VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[3507] ?? 'copper_sword',\n      VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[3509] ?? 'copper_pickaxe', VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[3506] ?? 'copper_axe'];\n    starter.forEach((key, i) => {\n      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[key];\n      if (id !== undefined) p.inv.slots[i] = { id, stack: 1 };\n    });\n  }\n\n\n  respawnPlayer() {\n    const p = this.player;\n    // 硬核角色已消亡（Player.cs:16894 difficulty==2 死亡后 ghost=true 不再重生；\n    // 本仓最小集 = hardcoreDead 标记 → 不允许重生，死亡画面停留）\n    if (p.appearance?.hardcoreDead) return;\n    p.hp = p.maxHp;"
}

  async function loadFromKey(key: string) {

      if (!text) { ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', '存档数据缺失')); return; }
      await loadFromJson(text);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      ui.hideProgress();
      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', (e as Error).message));
    }
  }

  loaded = false;

  /**
   * 启动初始化:加载语言列表 + 当前语言包。
   * lang 未指定时用 DEFAULT_LANG(zh-Hans);非法值归一到 en-US。
   */
  async init(lang?: string | number): Promise<boolean> {
    await this.loadIndex();
    return this.setLanguage(lang ?? 'zh-Hans');
  }

  private async loadIndex() {
    if (this.cultures.length) return;
    try {
      const r = await fetch('l10n/index.json');
      if (r.ok) this.cultures = await r.json();
    } catch { /* 列表缺失时仍可用 GameCulture 静态表 */
      if (!this.cultures.length) {
        const { CULTURES } = await import('./GameCulture');
        this.cultures = CULTURES;
      }
    }
  }

  /** 切换语言(culture 对象 | culture 名 | legacyId;LanguageManager.cs:54/60/92) */
  async setLanguage(culture: LangCultureInfo | string | number): Promise<boolean> {
    const name = typeof culture === 'object' ? culture.name : normalizeLang(culture);
    if (this.activeCulture?.name === name && this.loaded) return true;
    const tables = await this.fetchPack(name);
    if (!tables) return false;
    this.pack = tables;
    this.activeCulture = this.cultures.find((c) => c.name === name) ?? { name, legacyId: 0, displayNameKey: '' };
    this.loaded = true;
    this.warned.clear();
    this.emit();
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.dispatchEvent(new Event('sw:langchange'));
    return true;
  }

  private async fetchPack(name: string): Promise<LoadedPack | null> {
    const cached = this.packCache.get(name);
    if (cached) {
      this.packCache.delete(name);
      this.packCache.set(name, cached); // 刷新 LRU 序
      return cached;
    }
    try {
      const r = await fetch(`l10n/${name}.json`);
      if (!r.ok) return null;
      const pack = (await r.json()) as Pack;
      const tables = buildTables(pack);

import { runResetAndTerrain } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { runDunesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/DunesPass';
import { runOceanSandPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/BeachPass';
import { runSandPatchesPass, runTunnelsPass, runDirtWallBgPass, runSmallHolesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/MissingCavesPass';
import { runMountCavesPass, runMountainCavesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/MountCavesPass';
import { runRocksAndClayPass, runCavesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/CavesPass';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { fnv1a } from './pass-hash.test';

const V_ID = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const sheet = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof sheet === 'number') V_ID[i] = sheet;
}
function gh(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; w: number; h: number }): number {
  const ghost = new Uint16Array(st.w * st.h);
  for (let i = 0; i < ghost.length; i++) {
    if (st.flags[i]) continue;
    const t = st.type[i];
    if (t === 0) { ghost[i] = 0; continue; } // 内部空 → vanilla 0（与 oracle 裸 typ 同口径）
    const v = V_ID[t];
    ghost[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v;
  }
  return fnv1a(ghost);
}

5. 硬核消亡标记 validateAppearance。
6. Enemy 掉落 ctx：专家世界 EoC 宝袋 3319；经典世界无。
7. 存档往返 difficulty + journey。
8. wld 回填 master。
9. Journey 时间倍率。

For #6 mock game: need World with flags etc. World(64,32,...) has flags record default; groundLevel=0 fine.

Enemy.fromVanilla(4, x, y) — EoC def boss true. hurt(99999, 0, 0, game). The ctx needs game.dropContext?.() — optional. moonEventCtx?.() optional.

onEnemyKilled: (key, enemy) => {} required? It's called as game.onEnemyKilled(this.key, this) — GameHooks interface includes onEnemyKilled? Probably optional. Mock as no-op.

playSfx(name, vol, x, y) and playSfxFiles(list, ...). spawnParticles(x,y,color,n,speed,opts). addDamageNumber? In death path? There's `game.addDamageNumber` maybe on hit. I'll include no-ops for everything plausible.

For the spawnDrop recorder: return value used? `const drop = spawn(d.id, d.n)` and checks drop truthiness for gel coloring. Return null fine, but I need to record ids. Record args: id is item *key* — wait spawn() maps id→key: `const key = NATIVE_DROP_KEY[id] ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id]` then game.spawnDrop(x, y, key, n). So my recorder gets keys. For boss bag 3319 → VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[3319] key. I'll record the vanilla id by reversing: simpler to record keys and assert some key whose VANILLA id is 3319 — compute expected key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[3319] and assert recorder includes it.

Also careful: Enemy death for boss also spawns heart/potions etc. Fine.

Also EoC might have extra mechanics (spawning servants on death?) — doDeathEvents. Should be OK.

Note: `game.enemies()` used for twin suppression only for 125/126.

Let me check GameHooks interface to build a compliant mock.
```

6. packWorld test: after packWorld, w's buffers are detached; `World.fromPacket({...packet, buf: packet.buf})` — the buf arrays are ArrayBuffers already transferred? In test (no postMessage), they're fine.
   But note: fromPacket expects `buf.explored` optional — packet has no explored. Fine.
7. `loadSave(JSON.stringify(save))` for the wld test — save is version 2; fine.

Let me refactor the enemy kill helper to one function and simplify the starter assertion, then run tests.

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T08:31:07.669Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
The torch (custom item, internal id, not a vanilla id) — `VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[s.id]` uses the *internal id* as a vanilla id lookup! Bug: for custom items (torch etc.), internal id ≠ vanilla id. VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID maps vanilla item id → key. s.id is the internal id. For custom 'torch', internal id is small (like 5?) and VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[5] might be some other item's key or undefined. Then `?? ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.key` gives correct key only if the vanilla lookup missed. So the drop key can be WRONG (mapped to a different item) for internal ids that collide with vanilla ids.

Fix: prefer ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.key (always correct for internal ids), fall back to VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID only if the def has no key (shouldn't happen). Actually every internal id has a def with key. So: `const key = ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.key ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[s.id];`

Same pattern used in Enemy.ts spawn(): `NATIVE_DROP_KEY[id] ?? VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id]` — there `id` is a genuine vanilla item id from drop rules, so it's correct there. But in dropInventoryOnDeath, s.id is internal. Fix mine.
```

</details>
